# Omen DBS vNext Inventory

> **Status:** DRAFT audit evidence; non-authoritative until founder review.
> **Baseline:** `refactor/dbs-vnext-migration` at `065d4677d79c7ce9afe10b6cdb981d41f40a5471`.
> **Generated:** 2026-08-04T01:08:27.655Z.

## Scope and method

This inventory covers all 1,704 tracked paths at the pinned baseline commit and records every 773 relevant Markdown, HTML, YAML, JSON, prompt, agent, routing, schema, script/config, and deployment-boundary artifact selected by `scripts/build-inventory.mjs`. Audit files are outside that pinned commit and therefore cannot contaminate the source corpus.

Reference counts are deterministic direct-link/path detections, not a claim that prose contains no other semantic dependency. Dispositions are evidence-backed migration proposals using only the founder-approved values; `REVIEW` means the scan could not safely make a stronger call. No file was moved or deleted.

## Completeness summary

- Tracked files: **1,704**
- Relevant text/config artifacts inventoried: **773**
- Exact duplicate groups touching this repository: **1**
- Near-duplicate candidates touching this repository: **8**
- Broken or case-mismatched local-link findings: **139**
